# Negative-suite release findings (suite version 1)

Source: `build/negative/release.jsonl` (10,000 rows; 3526 windows with at least one L1–L5 claim; 0 ERROR outcomes). No thresholds, sync patterns, seeds, generator code, pipeline code, or native constants were changed in response. The two S6 L5 rows have now been traced in the linked writeup; other listed claims remain candidates for **separate scoped investigation**, not proven root causes.

The exact fixed-suite L5 failures are `S6_unsupported/949` and `S6_unsupported/1041` (entropy `[1,5,949]` and `[1,5,1041]`). A generic 1% rate-bound PASS in the JSON does not override the PRD's fixed-suite zero-confirmation FAIL.

Both L5 windows now have an evidence-backed [root-cause trace and structural fix proposal](negative_suite_s6_l5_root_cause.md). The full list below remains the unchanged version-1 release evidence.

The suite's operational L5 predicate accepts repeated structure plus a verified CRC stronger than CRC-8; `prd.md` additionally requires held-out evidence for frame confirmation. That semantic difference remains a release decision, not a reason to hide these two suite-level positives.

Reproduce one row through the production pipeline using this *audit-logged* one-line command, replacing `STRATUM` and `INDEX` from the table below. The first entropy component must remain suite version 1:

```bash
uv run python -c "import hashlib,json; from tools.corpus.evaluate import _audit; from tools.negative.run import ROOT,_one; s='STRATUM'; i=INDEX; _audit(ROOT,[f'negative-release-{s}-{i}'],'heldout',hashlib.sha256(b'negative-v1-release').hexdigest()); print(json.dumps(_one(('release',s,i)),sort_keys=True))"
```

The reproduction command creates a new release-suite audit invocation; do not treat a rerun as a fresh independent sample. The original checkpoint/report recorded one release-suite invocation at commit `c8ad052c8bfe0dd02acf65ab61b63604523c2764` with dirty=true.

## Root-cause hypotheses and frozen-generator caveats

- **S3 short-input claim (1 window):** unexpected confident classification/lock/frame on short data. The exact cause is unconfirmed; inspect the per-window pipeline result in a separate diagnostic task.
- **S6 OFDM-like unsupported input (1,025 windows with any claim):** both L5 windows were traced: the repeated 80-sample symbol evades the crest-factor part of the unsupported gate, 64-QAM lock remains mapping-unverified, HDLC flags form periodic triples, and the broad CRC-16/IBM sweep verifies spans crossing later header flags. Window 1041 repeats one byte-identical accidental CRC-valid word three times and is marked `AMBIGUOUS` despite L5. See the linked root-cause trace. The stratum still covers only one FFT size and a repeated symbol, not the originally requested OFDM diversity; other S6 claim causes remain hypotheses.
- **S7 nominal low-SNR lookalikes (1,250 windows with any claim):** `_qpsk` performs arithmetic on uint8 bits; a zero maps to 255 rather than −1, so signal power is far above the intended normalization and the added noise does not establish the advertised −5..3 dB SNR. Do not cite its 100% L1/L2 as evidence of low-SNR acquisition failure. Correct only in a new versioned suite and retain this v1 result.
- **S8 random framed PSK (1,250 windows with any claim):** the built-in sync-word rejection modifies `bits` *after* `x=_qpsk(bits)`; it cannot remove patterns from emitted samples. `_qpsk` also has the same uint8 underflow. Thus the 'no builtin sync' and ordinary-QPSK interpretations are unverified. Chance short-sync matches remain plausible, not established for a particular row.

## Complete window list

Each row records exact `(suite_version, stratum, index)` for regeneration and the observed ladder levels. Sections group by the hypotheses above. `INDEX` in the reproduction command is the integer in the first column. Statuses and scores remain in the JSONL for fuller triage.
